2026-11-21 20:47:18 INFO     [autogluon.assistant.agents.executer_agent] Error summary: The provided code defines a `build_model` function that returns an `AutoGluonWrapper` instance. However, the `AutoGluonWrapper` class uses `os.path.join(os.getcwd(), ...)` to create a directory for the model. In a cross-validation environment where multiple folds are executed, this can lead to path collisions or permission issues if the directory is not cleaned up correctly or if multiple processes attempt to access the same path. More importantly, the `__del__` method is unreliable for cleanup in Python, and the `fit` method is called repeatedly within the same process/environment. Additionally, the `AutoGluon` `path` parameter in `TabularPredictor` should ideally be managed by the evaluation harness or be unique per fold to avoid cross-contamination. The current implementation is risky for a 10-fold CV setup.

2026-11-21 20:53:46 INFO     [autogluon.assistant.agents.executer_agent] Error summary: The provided code defines a `build_model` function that returns an `AutoGluonWrapper` instance. However, the `AutoGluonWrapper` class uses `os.getcwd()` to define the `self.path` for the `TabularPredictor`. In a cross-validation loop, if multiple folds are processed in the same working directory, this can lead to file path collisions or permission errors when `shutil.rmtree` is called by the `__del__` method of a previous fold's object while a new fold is attempting to initialize. Furthermore, the `__del__` method is unreliable for cleanup in Python and may trigger errors if the directory is already removed or locked. The implementation should use a more robust temporary directory management strategy (e.g., `tempfile.mkdtemp`) and ensure that the `TabularPredictor` path is unique and handled safely.

However, the `AutoGluonWrapper` class uses `os.getcwd()` to define the `path` for the `TabularPredictor`. In a cross-validation loop, if multiple folds are processed or if the working directory is not managed correctly, this can lead to file system collisions or permission issues. More importantly, the `__del__` method attempts to `shutil.rmtree` the model directory. In many Python environments, `__del__` is not guaranteed to be called promptly or at all, and it can interfere with the `TabularPredictor`'s internal file handles if the object is still referenced or if the cleanup happens while the process is still active.
